```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  AxialCoordSchema,
  packAxialCell,
  unpackAxialCell,
  TopologyManifestSchema,
  type TopologyManifest,
} from '../fce/manifest.schema.js';
import { DynamicOverlaySchema } from '../fce/overlay.schema.js';
import { mergeOverlaySnapshot } from '../buz/overlay.buzz.js';

describe('38.types.unit - Pure Geometric Types & Manifest Validation', () => {
  describe('AxialCoordSchema & Cubic Invariant', () => {
    it('accepts valid coordinates satisfying q + r + s === 0', () => {
      const coord = { q: 10, r: -5, s: -5 };
      expect(AxialCoordSchema.parse(coord)).toEqual(coord);
    });

    it('rejects coordinates breaching cubic invariant', () => {
      expect(() => AxialCoordSchema.parse({ q: 1, r: 1, s: 0 })).toThrow(
        '[CUBIC_INVARIANT_BREACH]'
      );
    });

    it('rejects floating-point numbers in coordinates', () => {
      expect(() => AxialCoordSchema.parse({ q: 1.5, r: -1.5, s: 0 })).toThrow(
        '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED]'
      );
    });

    it('rejects coordinates exceeding bounds [-32768, 32767]', () => {
      expect(() => AxialCoordSchema.parse({ q: 32768, r: -32768, s: 0 })).toThrow(
        '[OUT_OF_BOUNDS]'
      );
    });

    it('rejects un-schemaed narrative properties via .strict()', () => {
      expect(() =>
        AxialCoordSchema.parse({ q: 0, r: 0, s: 0, actorId: 'char_bog' } as any)
      ).toThrow();
    });
  });

  describe('Axial Packing Bijection', () => {
    it('packs and unpacks origin correctly', () => {
      const packed = packAxialCell(0, 0);
      expect(packed).toBe(2147516416); // 0x80008000
      const unpacked = unpackAxialCell(packed);
      expect(unpacked).toEqual({ q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 });
    });

    it('preserves cubic invariant across corner extrema', () => {
      const corners = [
        [-32768, -32768],
        [-32768, 32767],
        [32767, -32768],
        [32767, 32767],
      ];
      for (const [q, r] of corners) {
        const p = packAxialCell(q, r);
        const u = unpackAxialCell(p);
        expect(u.q).toBe(q);
        expect(u.r).toBe(r);
        expect(u.q + u.r + u.s).toBe(0);
      }
    });
  });

  describe('Topology Manifest & Dynamic Overlay Merger', () => {
    const dummyManifest: TopologyManifest = {
      topologyId: 'loc_great_hall',
      schemaVersion: 'v1.0',
      merkleRootHex: 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855',
      cells: [
        {
          coord: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
          packedId: 2147516416,
          costUnits: 100,
          passable: true,
        },
      ],
      portals: [
        {
          portalId: 'portal_archway',
          fromRoomId: 'loc_great_hall',
          toRoomId: 'loc_courtyard',
          entryCoord: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
          exitCoord: { q: 1, r: -1, s: 0 },
          acousticDampingCdb: 500,
          traversableDefault: false,
        },
      ],
      occluders: [
        {
          occluderId: 'occ_iron_gate',
          coord: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
          blocksLoSDefault: true,
          attenuationCdb: 1200,
        },
      ],
    };

    it('validates a schema-compliant topology manifest', () => {
      expect(TopologyManifestSchema.parse(dummyManifest)).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('merges dynamic overlay without mutating source manifest', () => {
      const overlay = DynamicOverlaySchema.parse({
        overlayId: 'ovl_stage_1',
        parentTopologyHash: 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855',
        occluderOverrides: { occ_iron_gate: false },
        portalOverrides: { portal_archway: true },
        attenuationDeltasCdb: { occ_iron_gate: -400 },
      });

      const snapshot = mergeOverlaySnapshot(dummyManifest, overlay);

      expect(snapshot.portals[0].traversable).toBe(true);
      expect(snapshot.occluders[0].blocksLoS).toBe(false);
      expect(snapshot.occluders[0].attenuationCdb).toBe(800);

      // Verify source immutability
      expect(dummyManifest.portals[0].traversableDefault).toBe(false);
      expect(dummyManifest.occluders[0].blocksLoSDefault).toBe(true);
      expect(dummyManifest.occluders[0].attenuationCdb).toBe(1200);
      expect(Object.isFrozen(snapshot)).toBe(true);
    });
  });
});
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### User Story

In the low-lit avionics bay of Rig 1 at 3:14 AM, Lead Architect Brad was auditing a split-brain divergence that had corrupted a long-running simulation cycle. Two combatants in the subterranean foundry—Bog and Spleen—had triggered an opposed grapple encounter at what the simulation ledger claimed was identical coordinates $(q=4, r=-2)$. Downstream, the language generator had hallucinated a scene where Bog vaulted over a heavy iron blast hatch to disarm Spleen. But when the audio engine measured speech duration and the timeline committed back to disk, the entire physics state had fractured: the hatch was recorded as shut in the local database, yet the line-of-sight raycaster had allowed light and movement directly through the closed barrier.

The post-mortem revealed that an upstream adapter had passed an unvalidated, floating-point coordinate ($q=4.0000000002$) alongside a dynamic door state that omitted its target portal identifier. Because the geometry layer lacked strict schema boundaries, the engine had cast the float into memory, rounded it unpredictably across CPU threads, and bypassed the topological occluder check.

With **Phase 1 Pure Geometric Types Validation In 38.types.unit** active, that failure mode is physically impossible. The moment any entity, adapter, or rogue agent attempts to submit a coordinate with fractional decimals, an axial triad that breaches the zero-sum law $q + r + s = 0$, or an overlay containing un-schemaed narrative properties, `38.types.unit` fails closed in microsecond time at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$. Static topology is locked behind content-addressed SHA-256 Merkle roots, while dynamic door latches and barrier dampers merge into immutable memory snapshots with mathematical precision. Brad watches the terminal screen stream green passes across the entire lattice: space is no longer an approximation—it is an unyielding, compile-time proof.

#### Strategic Goal

Supports the foundational mandate of **Zero-Direct-Ingress Deterministic Spatial Causality**: enforcing strict compile-time geometric closure and synchronous sub-millisecond execution ($< 2\text{ ms}$) at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$, guaranteeing that narrative intent, character lore, or IEEE-754 floating-point drift can never cross the physical boundary into the spatial oracle.

#### Context & Value

- **The Strategic Value:** Establishes the authoritative mathematical bedrock for `000.goblin-spatial`. By separating "what space means" (the canon dossiers in `000.goblin-lore`), "who is where" (the event-sourced ledger in `000.series-sower-engine`), and "how space works" (the stateless oracle in `000.goblin-spatial`), the pipeline prevents generative AI hallucinations and split-brain desynchronization across multi-year narrative arcs.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Designing zero-cost, type-safe axial packing utilities (`packAxialCell` / `unpackAxialCell`) that eliminate JavaScript signed 32-bit shift sign-inversion bugs, while composing strict `.strict()` Zod schemas and pure, immutable overlay mergers that execute purely in V8 memory without generating garbage collection pauses or allocating native I/O handles.

#### Interface

- **`001.goblin-lore` (Upstream Canon Repository):** Compiles static spatial dossiers (`locations/*.md`) into validated `TopologyManifest` JSON artifacts, stamping each room with a canonical SHA-256 Merkle root (`merkleRootHex`) and pre-calculated acoustic attenuation metrics.
- **`000.series-sower-engine` (Runtime Simulation Ledger):** Ingests compiled topology manifests, tracks dynamic portal states (open/closed) in SQLite tables, sanitizes character states down to bare coordinates and integer movement budgets, and passes transient `DynamicOverlay` payloads across the synchronous `SpatialPort` boundary.
- **`000.goblin-spatial` (`packages/301.spatial` Internal Units):**
  - `14.port.unit`: Ingests `AxialCoordSchema` and `TopologyManifestSchema` to validate incoming raw queries before invoking the geometry kernel.
  - `19.ledger.unit` & `42.receipt.unit`: Ingests `Sha256HashSchema` and `PackedAxial` to construct immutable cryptographic audit quadruples.
  - `43.path.unit` & `13.sound.unit`: Ingests `MergedTopologySnapshot` to calculate budget-bounded A* paths and acoustic wavefront decay over merged topological graphs.

#### Interaction

- **Blessed Terminal Interface (Cockpit Switchboard):**
  - In the 12x12 curses cockpit (`packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit`), selecting `[01] LOAD CANONICAL TOPOLOGY MANIFEST` or `[SCHEMA MENU] -> AUDIT GEOMETRIC TYPE CONTRACTS` dispatches an audit action to `38.types.unit`.
  - The buzzer exercises the schema suite against synthetic boundary conditions (origin $(0,0,0)$, corner extrema $[-32768, 32767]$, float injection $1.5$, and cubic breach $q+r+s \neq 0$).

Zero `Promise` allocations, zero `async`/`await`, zero network sockets, and zero `setTimeout` delays on geometric queries. Queries must execute purely in-memory within the local V8 isolate heap in nanoseconds.
4. **ZERO DIRECT INGRESS (THE EPISTEMIC FIREWALL):** Query payloads must accept only packed integer axial coordinates, structural bitmasks, and integer budgets. Character lore, IDs, names, trauma strings, emotional statuses, and narrative prompts are strictly forbidden from crossing into the spatial engine.
```typescript
import type { Model } from '../../99.core/interface/model.interface.js';
import type { AxialCoord, PackedAxial } from '../../38.types.unit/fce/manifest.schema.js';

export type SpatialQueryType = 'RAYCAST' | 'BOUNDED_PATH' | 'REACHABILITY' | 'DISPLACEMENT';

export type RaycastMode = 'SUBTRACTIVE_LINEAR' | 'MULTIPLICATIVE_BEER_LAMBERT';

export interface ScrubbedRaycastQuery {
  readonly queryType: 'RAYCAST';
  readonly origin: AxialCoord;
  readonly target: AxialCoord;
  readonly mode: RaycastMode;
}

export interface ScrubbedRoutingQuery {
  readonly queryType: 'BOUNDED_PATH';
  readonly origin: AxialCoord;
  readonly destination: AxialCoord;
  readonly maxBudgetCostUnits: number;
  readonly workEnvelopeLimit: number;
}

export interface ScrubbedReachabilityQuery {
  readonly queryType: 'REACHABILITY';
  readonly origin: AxialCoord;
  readonly maxStepRadius: number;
  readonly workEnvelopeLimit: number;
}

export interface ScrubbedDisplacementQuery {
  readonly queryType: 'DISPLACEMENT';
  readonly targetCell: AxialCoord;
  readonly maxCellCapacity: number;
  readonly maxBudgetCostUnits: number;
}

export type ScrubbedSpatialQuery =
  | ScrubbedRaycastQuery
  | ScrubbedRoutingQuery
  | ScrubbedReachabilityQuery
  | ScrubbedDisplacementQuery;

export interface SpatialExecutionContext {
  readonly executionId: string;
  readonly sequenceNumber: number;
  readonly tickNumber: number;
  readonly topologyHash: string;
  readonly overlayHash: string;
}

export interface SynchronousExecutionReceipt<T = unknown> {
  readonly ok: boolean;
  readonly queryType: SpatialQueryType;
  readonly kernelVersion: string;
  readonly payload: T;
  readonly cellsExpanded: number;
  readonly failureCode?: 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED' | 'UNREACHABLE' | 'OCCLUDED' | 'SCHEMA_BREACH';
  readonly executionMicros: number;
}

export interface SpatialPort {
  readonly kernelVersion: string;
  invokeSynchronous<T = unknown>(
    rawQuery: unknown,
    context?: SpatialExecutionContext
  ): SynchronousExecutionReceipt<T>;
}

export interface QueryStripValidationResult {
  readonly valid: boolean;
  readonly scrubbedQuery?: ScrubbedSpatialQuery;
  readonly strippedKeys: readonly string[];
  readonly breachReason?: string;
}

export default interface Port extends Model {
  idx: string;
  kernelVersion: string;
  totalQueriesProcessed: number;
  totalBudgetsTripped: number;
  lastExecutionMicros: number;
}
```

#### 2. `packages/301.spatial/14.port.unit/val/port.model.ts`

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';
import { AxialCoordSchema } from '../../38.types.unit/fce/manifest.schema.js';

export const BANNED_NARRATIVE_TAGS = Object.freeze([
  'actorId',
  'characterId',
  'characterName',
  'name',
  'dialogue',
  'speech',
  'trauma',
  'wound',
  'emotion',
  'inventory',
  'item',
  'relationship',
  'grudge',
  'intent',
  'lore',
  'prose',
]);

export const StrictRaycastQuerySchema = z
  .object({
    queryType: z.literal('RAYCAST'),
    origin: AxialCoordSchema,
    target: AxialCoordSchema,
    mode: z.enum(['SUBTRACTIVE_LINEAR', 'MULTIPLICATIVE_BEER_LAMBERT']),
  })
  .strict();

export const StrictRoutingQuerySchema = z
  .object({
    queryType: z.literal('BOUNDED_PATH'),
    origin: AxialCoordSchema,
    destination: AxialCoordSchema,
    maxBudgetCostUnits: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] maxBudgetCostUnits must be an integer' })
      .positive({ message: '[BUDGET_INVALID] maxBudgetCostUnits must be positive' }),
    workEnvelopeLimit: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] workEnvelopeLimit must be an integer' })
      .positive()
      .max(2048, { message: '[WORK_LIMIT_EXCEEDED] Maximum work envelope ceiling is 2048' })
      .default(512),
  })
  .strict();

export const StrictReachabilityQuerySchema = z
  .object({
    queryType: z.literal('REACHABILITY'),
    origin: AxialCoordSchema,
    maxStepRadius: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] maxStepRadius must be an integer' })
      .positive()
      .max(128, { message: '[RADIUS_LIMIT_EXCEEDED] Maximum reachability radius is 128' }),
    workEnvelopeLimit: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] workEnvelopeLimit must be an integer' })
      .positive()
      .max(2048)
      .default(512),
  })
  .strict();

export const StrictDisplacementQuerySchema = z
  .object({
    queryType: z.literal('DISPLACEMENT'),
    targetCell: AxialCoordSchema,
    maxCellCapacity: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] maxCellCapacity must be an integer' })
      .positive(),
    maxBudgetCostUnits: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] maxBudgetCostUnits must be an integer' })
      .positive(),
  })
  .strict();

export const SpatialQueryDiscriminatorSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('queryType', [
  StrictRaycastQuerySchema,
  StrictRoutingQuerySchema,
  StrictReachabilityQuerySchema,
  StrictDisplacementQuerySchema,
]);

export type ValidatedSpatialQuery = z.infer<typeof SpatialQueryDiscriminatorSchema>;
```

#### 6. `packages/301.spatial/14.port.unit/buz/port.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import { PortModel } from '../port.model.js';
import type PortBit from '../fce/port.bit.js';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import {
  QueryStripValidationResult,
  ScrubbedSpatialQuery,
  SynchronousExecutionReceipt,
} from '../fce/port.interface.js';
import {
  BANNED_NARRATIVE_TAGS,
  SpatialQueryDiscriminatorSchema,
} from '../val/port.model.js';

/**
 * Epistemic Firewall: Recursively sanitizes query payloads by stripping narrative lore tags.
 * Rejects payloads if banned values represent prose contamination.
 */
export function sanitizeSpatialQueryPayload(rawPayload: unknown): QueryStripValidationResult {
  if (rawPayload === null || typeof rawPayload !== 'object') {
    return {
      valid: false,
      strippedKeys: [],
      breachReason: '[SCHEMA_BREACH] Raw payload must be a non-null object',
    };
  }

  const strippedKeys: string[] = [];
  const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rawPayload));

  function scrubNode(node: Record<string, any>): void {
    for (const key of Object.keys(node)) {
      if (BANNED_NARRATIVE_TAGS.includes(key as any)) {
        delete node[key];
        strippedKeys.push(key);
      } else if (typeof node[key] === 'object' && node[key] !== null) {
        scrubNode(node[key]);
      }
    }
  }

  scrubNode(cloned);

  const parsed = SpatialQueryDiscriminatorSchema.safeParse(cloned);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      strippedKeys: Object.freeze(strippedKeys),
      breachReason: `[SCHEMA_BREACH] ${parsed.error.errors.map((e) => e.message).join('; ')}`,
    };
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    scrubbedQuery: Object.freeze(parsed.data) as ScrubbedSpatialQuery,
    strippedKeys: Object.freeze(strippedKeys),
  };
}

/**
 * Deterministic Bounded Work Envelope Guard.
 */
export function checkWorkBudgetGuard(
  cellsExpanded: number,
  workEnvelopeLimit: number
): { tripped: boolean; failureCode?: 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED' } {
  if (cellsExpanded >= workEnvelopeLimit) {
    return { tripped: true, failureCode: 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED' };
  }
  return { tripped: false };
}

export const initPort = (
  cpy: PortModel,
  bal?: PortBit,
  ste?: State
): PortModel => {
  cpy.totalQueriesProcessed = 0;
  cpy.totalBudgetsTripped = 0;
  cpy.lastExecutionMicros = 0;

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ prtBit: { idx: 'init-port', val: 1, src: cpy.kernelVersion } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const sanitizeQuery = (
  cpy: PortModel,
  bal?: PortBit,
  ste?: State
): PortModel => {
  const result = sanitizeSpatialQueryPayload(bal?.dat?.query);

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      prtBit: {
        idx: 'sanitize-query',
        val: result.valid ? 1 : 0,
        dat: {
          scrubbedQuery: result.scrubbedQuery,
          error: result.breachReason,
        },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const executeQuery = (
  cpy: PortModel,
  bal?: PortBit,
  ste?: State
): PortModel => {
  const startHr = typeof performance !== 'undefined' ? performance.now() : Date.now();
  const rawQuery = bal?.dat?.query;

  const sanitizeResult = sanitizeSpatialQueryPayload(rawQuery);
  if (!sanitizeResult.valid || !sanitizeResult.scrubbedQuery) {
    const elapsed = Math.round(((typeof performance !== 'undefined' ? performance.now() : Date.now()) - startHr) * 1000);
    cpy.lastExecutionMicros = elapsed;

    const receipt: SynchronousExecutionReceipt = {
      ok: false,
      queryType: (rawQuery as any)?.queryType ?? 'RAYCAST',
      kernelVersion: cpy.kernelVersion,
      payload: null,
      cellsExpanded: 0,
      failureCode: 'SCHEMA_BREACH',
      executionMicros: elapsed,
    };

    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({ prtBit: { idx: 'execute-query-fail', val: 0, dat: { receipt, error: sanitizeResult.breachReason } } });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const query = sanitizeResult.scrubbedQuery;
  const envelopeLimit = (query as any).workEnvelopeLimit ?? 512;

  // Evaluate execution within bounded synchronous envelopes
  let cellsExpanded = 0;
  let ok = true;
  let failureCode: SynchronousExecutionReceipt['failureCode'];

  if (query.queryType === 'BOUNDED_PATH') {
    const dx = Math.abs(query.destination.q - query.origin.q);
    const dy = Math.abs(query.destination.r - query.origin.r);
    cellsExpanded = (dx + dy) * 10;

    const budgetTrip = checkWorkBudgetGuard(cellsExpanded, envelopeLimit);
    if (budgetTrip.tripped) {
      ok = false;
      failureCode = 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED';
      cpy.totalBudgetsTripped += 1;
    }
  }

  cpy.totalQueriesProcessed += 1;
  const elapsedMicros = Math.round(((typeof performance !== 'undefined' ? performance.now() : Date.now()) - startHr) * 1000);
  cpy.lastExecutionMicros = elapsedMicros;

  const receipt: SynchronousExecutionReceipt = Object.freeze({
    ok,
    queryType: query.queryType,
    kernelVersion: cpy.kernelVersion,
    payload: ok ? { origin: query.origin } : null,
    cellsExpanded,
    failureCode,
    executionMicros: elapsedMicros,
  });

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ prtBit: { idx: 'execute-query-success', val: ok ? 1 : 0, dat: { receipt } } });
  }
  return cpy;
};
```

#### 8. `packages/301.spatial/14.port.unit/port.reduce.ts`

```typescript
import clone from 'clone-deep';
import * as Act from './port.action.js';
import { PortModel } from './port.model.js';
import * as Buzz from './port.buzzer.js';
import type State from '../99.core/state.js';

export function reducer(
  model: PortModel = new PortModel(),
  act: Act.Actions,
  state?: State
) {
  switch (act.type) {
    case Act.INIT_PORT:
      return Buzz.initPort(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.EXECUTE_QUERY:
      return Buzz.executeQuery(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.SANITIZE_QUERY:
      return Buzz.sanitizeQuery(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    default:
      return model;
  }
}
```

#### 10. `packages/301.spatial/14.port.unit/test/port.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { PortModel } from '../port.model.js';
import {
  sanitizeSpatialQueryPayload,
  checkWorkBudgetGuard,
  initPort,
  executeQuery,
} from '../buz/port.buzz.js';
import type PortBit from '../fce/port.bit.js';

describe('14.port.unit - Synchronous Spatial Port Interface', () => {
  let model: PortModel;

  beforeEach(() => {
    model = new PortModel();
    initPort(model);
  });

  describe('Epistemic Firewall (Zero Direct Ingress)', () => {
    it('strips banned narrative tags from input query', () => {
      const rawPayload = {
        queryType: 'RAYCAST',
        origin: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
        target: { q: 5, r: -5, s: 0 },
        mode: 'SUBTRACTIVE_LINEAR',
        actorId: 'char_bog',
        characterName: 'Bog',
        trauma: 'CRUSHED_HAND',
        dialogue: 'Get out of my way',
      };

      const result = sanitizeSpatialQueryPayload(rawPayload);
      expect(result.valid).toBe(true);
      expect(result.strippedKeys).toContain('actorId');
      expect(result.strippedKeys).toContain('characterName');
      expect(result.strippedKeys).toContain('trauma');
      expect(result.strippedKeys).toContain('dialogue');
      expect((result.scrubbedQuery as any).actorId).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('rejects payloads with invalid axial coordinates or floating points', () => {
      const rawPayload = {
        queryType: 'RAYCAST',
        origin: { q: 1.5, r: -1.5, s: 0 },
        target: { q: 5, r: -5, s: 0 },
        mode: 'SUBTRACTIVE_LINEAR',
      };

      const result = sanitizeSpatialQueryPayload(rawPayload);
      expect(result.valid).toBe(false);
      expect(result.breachReason).toContain('[FLOAT_PROHIBITED]');
    });

    it('rejects queries failing cubic invariant q + r + s === 0', () => {
      const rawPayload = {
        queryType: 'BOUNDED_PATH',
        origin: { q: 1, r: 1, s: 0 },
        destination: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
        maxBudgetCostUnits: 100,
      };

      const result = sanitizeSpatialQueryPayload(rawPayload);
      expect(result.valid).toBe(false);
      expect(result.breachReason).toContain('[CUBIC_INVARIANT_BREACH]');
    });
  });

  describe('Bounded Work Envelope Tripwires', () => {
    it('permits execution within safe work envelope limits', () => {
      const check = checkWorkBudgetGuard(250, 512);
      expect(check.tripped).toBe(false);
      expect(check.failureCode).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('trips BUDGET_EXCEEDED when cells expanded reach work envelope ceiling', () => {
      const check = checkWorkBudgetGuard(512, 512);
      expect(check.tripped).toBe(true);
      expect(check.failureCode).toBe('BUDGET_EXCEEDED');
    });
  });

  describe('Synchronous Execution & Zero Promise Allocation', () => {
    it('executes synchronously returning deep-frozen receipts', () => {
      let resolvedReceipt: any = null;

      const bale: PortBit = {
        idx: 'query-1',
        dat: {
          query: {
            queryType: 'RAYCAST',
            origin: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
            target: { q: 2, r: -2, s: 0 },
            mode: 'SUBTRACTIVE_LINEAR',
          },
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          resolvedReceipt = res.prtBit.dat?.receipt;
        },
      };

      const ret = executeQuery(model, bale);

      // Verify synchronous execution (not a Promise)
      expect(ret).not.toBeInstanceOf(Promise);
      expect(resolvedReceipt).toBeDefined();
      expect(resolvedReceipt.ok).toBe(true);
      expect(resolvedReceipt.queryType).toBe('RAYCAST');
      expect(Object.isFrozen(resolvedReceipt)).toBe(true);
    });

    it('fails closed and updates budget trip counter on envelope breach', () => {
      let resolvedReceipt: any = null;

      const bale: PortBit = {
        idx: 'path-query-trip',
        dat: {
          query: {
            queryType: 'BOUNDED_PATH',
            origin: { q: -50, r: 50, s: 0 },
            destination: { q: 50, r: -50, s: 0 },
            maxBudgetCostUnits: 500,
            workEnvelopeLimit: 256,
          },
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          resolvedReceipt = res.prtBit.dat?.receipt;
        },
      };

      executeQuery(model, bale);

      expect(resolvedReceipt.ok).toBe(false);
      expect(resolvedReceipt.failureCode).toBe('BUDGET_EXCEEDED');
      expect(model.totalBudgetsTripped).toBe(1);
    });
  });
});
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### User Story

In the cold, humming server stacks of Rig 1, Sower Engine tick worker 03 had locked up hard during an opposed duel simulation between the foundry characters Bog and Spleen. The simulation ledger had ground to a halt: downstream scenes were stalled, the director's writer desk showed frozen spinners, and CPU Core 7 was pegged at 100% utilization. A rogue language model adapter had attempted to resolve an obstacle check by sending a 4-kilobyte JSON payload directly into the spatial resolver. Embedded deep inside the query were prompt strings, character psychological grudge notes, and an unbounded path request across an unmeshed cavern. The spatial solver had tried to deserialize the narrative text as coordinate data, threw an unhandled asynchronous rejection inside a microtask queue, and starved the main event loop.

With **Phase 2 Synchronous Spatial Port Interface In 14.port.unit** mounted, that vulnerability is eliminated at the threshold. When the simulation engine needs to know if Bog can see Spleen through an iron grate, it encounters the `SpatialPort` boundary. The port acts as an impenetrable airlock: it strips away the character names, grievances, and emotional modifiers, discarding them into a quarantine log. It accepts only verified integer axial coordinates, clamps the path budget to an immutable 512-cell work envelope, and executes the raycast purely within the local V8 heap in 42 microseconds. The result returns as a sealed, deep-frozen receipt with an explicit integer cost. The event loop never yields, no promises are allocated, and the simulation advances with uninterrupted clockwork precision.

#### Strategic Goal

Supports the absolute system directive of **Sub-Millisecond Synchronous Spatial Determinism ($< 1\text{ ms}$)** at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$. It eliminates asynchronous microtask pauses, prevents V8 event loop starvation, and strictly decouples narrative storytelling from stateless geometric and acoustic reality.

#### Context & Value

- **The Strategic Value:** Creates the formal, air-gapped contractual bridge between narrative intention and physical possibility. By guaranteeing that the spatial oracle cannot see or understand characters, dialogue, or plot, the architecture mathematically guarantees that physical laws cannot be warped or hallucinated by language models.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Constructing a synchronous, zero-promise execution interface that validates complex discriminated schemas with Zod, scrubs deep JSON objects recursively for banned narrative keys, and enforces hard algorithmic expansion ceilings (work envelopes) without inducing garbage collector thrashing or dropping execution throughput below 10,000 queries per second.

#### Interface

- **`000.series-sower-engine` (Runtime Simulation Ledger):** Translates high-level character movements and perception requests into bare integer queries, passing them across the `SpatialPort` boundary to receive immutable `SynchronousExecutionReceipt` objects containing path lengths and sensory proofs.
- **`packages/301.spatial/38.types.unit` (Upstream Geometric Types):** Supplies `AxialCoordSchema`, `PackedAxial` encoders, and `MergedTopologySnapshot` to validate coordinate parameters and topological boundaries.
- **`packages/301.spatial/00.spatial.unit` (Downstream Geometric Kernel):** Ingests the scrubbed, validated queries emitted by `14.port.unit` and executes the underlying A* search, raycast intersection, and acoustic breadth-first sweeps.
- **`packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit` (Cryptographic Proof Sealer):** Consumes the execution outputs from `14.port.unit` to assemble four-part audit quadruples ($H_{\text{topology}}, H_{\text{overlay}}, H_{\text{query}}, H_{\text{receipt}}$).

#### Interaction

- **Blessed Terminal Interface (TUI Flight Deck):**
  - In the curses cockpit (`apps/995.library` running `packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit`), selecting `[02] RUN SYNCHRONOUS PORT PROBE` dispatches `EXECUTE_QUERY` across `ste.hunt`.
  - The menu buzzer feeds synthetic test queries (a valid line-of-sight raycast, a contaminated narrative query with banned tags, and a runaway path query exceeding envelope limits) through `14.port.unit`.
  - Console pane `cns00` renders real-time execution receipts in pure 7-bit ASCII:
    ```plaintext
    >> [SPATIAL::PORT] Probing 14.port.unit synchronous execution gate...
    >> [PORT::FIREWALL] Submitting contaminated query with actorId & trauma...
    >> [PORT::SCRUB] Banned keys stripped: ['actorId', 'trauma'] [PASS]
    >> [PORT::ENVELOPE] Testing path request beyond 512 cells...
    >> [PORT::TRIPWIRE] Bounded ceiling tripped: BUDGET_EXCEEDED (26 micros) [PASS]
    >> [PORT::SYNCHRONOUS] Verified 0 Promise allocations across 1,000 queries.
    >> [PORT::STATUS] SpatialPort certified online and pure.
    ```
  - If a query permits an un-schemaed key or returns a Promise, an immediate red fault indicator (`:: [FAIL] [EPISTEMIC_FIREWALL_BREACH]`) appears in `cns00`, halting test gauntlets before bad code can reach staging.

#### 2. `packages/301.spatial/19.ledger.unit/val/ledger.model.ts`

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';

export const GENESIS_HASH_64 = '0'.repeat(64);

export const Sha256HashSchema = z
  .string()
  .length(64, { message: '[HASH_LENGTH_BREACH] SHA-256 hash must be exactly 64 characters' })
  .regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/, {
    message: '[HASH_FORMAT_BREACH] SHA-256 hash must be lowercase hexadecimal',
  });

export const SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema = z
  .object({
    topologyHash: Sha256HashSchema,
    overlayHash: Sha256HashSchema,
    queryHash: Sha256HashSchema,
    receiptHash: Sha256HashSchema,
  })
  .strict();

export const SpatialLedgerEntrySchema = z
  .object({
    sequenceNumber: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] sequenceNumber must be an integer' })
      .positive({ message: '[SEQUENCE_INVALID] sequenceNumber must be positive' }),
    tickNumber: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] tickNumber must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative({ message: '[TICK_INVALID] tickNumber cannot be negative' }),
    streamId: z.string().min(1, { message: '[STREAM_ID_EMPTY] streamId cannot be empty' }),
    queryType: z.enum(['RAYCAST', 'BOUNDED_PATH', 'REACHABILITY', 'DISPLACEMENT']),
    auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema,
    kernelVersion: z.string().min(1),
    prevEventHash: Sha256HashSchema,
    chainedEventHash: Sha256HashSchema,
    recordedAtMs: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  })
  .strict();

export type ValidatedSpatialAuditQuadruple = z.infer<typeof SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema>;
export type ValidatedSpatialLedgerEntry = z.infer<typeof SpatialLedgerEntrySchema>;
```

#### 6. `packages/301.spatial/19.ledger.unit/buz/ledger.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { LedgerModel } from '../ledger.model.js';
import type LedgerBit from '../fce/ledger.bit.js';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type {
  SpatialAuditQuadruple,
  SpatialLedgerEntry,
  LineageVerificationResult,
} from '../fce/ledger.interface.js';
import {
  GENESIS_HASH_64,
  SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema,
  SpatialLedgerEntrySchema,
} from '../val/ledger.model.js';

/**
 * RFC 8785 JSON Canonicalization Scheme (JCS) Serializer.
 * Enforces lexicographical key sorting by UTF-16 code units and Unicode NFC normalization.
 */
export function canonicalizeJson(obj: unknown): string {
  if (obj === null) return 'null';

  if (typeof obj === 'number') {
    if (!Number.isFinite(obj)) {
      throw new TypeError('[CANONICALIZE_ERROR] Non-finite IEEE-754 numbers are prohibited');
    }
    return obj.toString();
  }

  if (typeof obj === 'boolean') {
    return obj ? 'true' : 'false';
  }

  if (typeof obj === 'string') {
    return JSON.stringify(obj.normalize('NFC'));
  }

  if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
    return `[${obj.map((el) => canonicalizeJson(el)).join(',')}]`;
  }

  if (typeof obj === 'object') {
    const sortedKeys = Object.keys(obj as Record<string, unknown>).sort((a, b) => {
      const minLen = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < minLen; i++) {
        const codeA = a.charCodeAt(i);
        const codeB = b.charCodeAt(i);
        if (codeA !== codeB) return codeA - codeB;
      }
      return a.length - b.length;
    });

    const entries = sortedKeys.map(
      (key) => `${JSON.stringify(key.normalize('NFC'))}:${canonicalizeJson((obj as any)[key])}`
    );
    return `{${entries.join(',')}}`;
  }

  throw new TypeError(`[CANONICALIZE_ERROR] Unsupported datatype: ${typeof obj}`);
}

export function computeSha256Hex(content: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(content, 'utf8').digest('hex');
}

/**
 * Computes chained event hash: SHA-256(prevEventHash || CanonicalJCS(entryWithoutHash)).
 */
export function sealLedgerEntry(
  entryWithoutHash: Omit<SpatialLedgerEntry, 'chainedEventHash'>
): SpatialLedgerEntry {
  const canonicalPayload = canonicalizeJson(entryWithoutHash);
  const chainedEventHash = computeSha256Hex(`${entryWithoutHash.prevEventHash}${canonicalPayload}`);

  return Object.freeze({
    ...entryWithoutHash,
    chainedEventHash,
  });
}

/**
 * Validates cryptographic lineage across entire recorded ledger entries.
 */
export function verifyLedgerLineage(entries: readonly SpatialLedgerEntry[]): LineageVerificationResult {
  if (entries.length === 0) {
    return { valid: true, totalEntries: 0 };
  }

  let expectedPrevHash = GENESIS_HASH_64;

  for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
    const entry = entries[i];
    const expectedSequence = i + 1;

    if (entry.sequenceNumber !== expectedSequence) {
      return {
        valid: false,
        totalEntries: entries.length,
        brokenSequenceIndex: i,
        failureReason: `[SEQUENCE_DISCONTINUITY] Expected sequence ${expectedSequence}, found ${entry.sequenceNumber}`,
      };
    }

    if (entry.prevEventHash !== expectedPrevHash) {
      return {
        valid: false,
        totalEntries: entries.length,
        brokenSequenceIndex: i,
        expectedHash: expectedPrevHash,
        actualHash: entry.prevEventHash,
        failureReason: `[PREV_HASH_MISMATCH] Entry ${i + 1} prevEventHash does not match expected lineage`,
      };
    }

    const { chainedEventHash, ...payloadWithoutHash } = entry;
    const recalculatedHash = computeSha256Hex(`${entry.prevEventHash}${canonicalizeJson(payloadWithoutHash)}`);

    if (recalculatedHash !== chainedEventHash) {
      return {
        valid: false,
        totalEntries: entries.length,
        brokenSequenceIndex: i,
        expectedHash: recalculatedHash,
        actualHash: chainedEventHash,
        failureReason: `[CHAIN_HASH_CORRUPTION] Entry ${i + 1} hash tampering detected`,
      };
    }

    expectedPrevHash = chainedEventHash;
  }

  return { valid: true, totalEntries: entries.length };
}

export const initLedger = (
  cpy: LedgerModel,
  bal?: LedgerBit,
  ste?: State
): LedgerModel => {
  cpy.headSequence = 0;
  cpy.lastEventHash = GENESIS_HASH_64;
  cpy.totalEntriesCommitted = 0;
  cpy.entries = Object.freeze([]);

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ ldgBit: { idx: 'init-ledger-success', val: 1 } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const appendEntry = (
  cpy: LedgerModel,
  bal?: LedgerBit,
  ste?: State
): LedgerModel => {
  const quadruple = bal?.dat?.auditQuadruple;
  const queryType = bal?.dat?.queryType ?? 'RAYCAST';
  const tickNumber = bal?.dat?.tickNumber ?? 0;
  const streamId = bal?.dat?.streamId ?? 'spatial-default-stream';

  const quadParsed = SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema.safeParse(quadruple);
  if (!quadParsed.success) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        ldgBit: {
          idx: 'append-entry-fail',
          val: 0,
          dat: { error: `[QUADRUPLE_SCHEMA_BREACH] ${quadParsed.error.errors.map((e) => e.message).join('; ')}` },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const nextSequence = cpy.headSequence + 1;
  const unsealedEntry = {
    sequenceNumber: nextSequence,
    tickNumber,
    streamId,
    queryType,
    auditQuadruple: quadParsed.data,
    kernelVersion: 'v1.0.0-spatial-oracle',
    prevEventHash: cpy.lastEventHash,
    recordedAtMs: Date.now(),
  };

  const sealedEntry = sealLedgerEntry(unsealedEntry);
  const validatedEntry = SpatialLedgerEntrySchema.parse(sealedEntry);

  const updatedEntries = [...cpy.entries, validatedEntry];
  cpy.entries = Object.freeze(updatedEntries);
  cpy.headSequence = nextSequence;
  cpy.lastEventHash = sealedEntry.chainedEventHash;
  cpy.totalEntriesCommitted = updatedEntries.length;

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      ldgBit: {
        idx: 'append-entry-success',
        val: 1,
        dat: { entry: validatedEntry },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const verifyLineage = (
  cpy: LedgerModel,
  bal?: LedgerBit,
  ste?: State
): LedgerModel => {
  const result = verifyLedgerLineage(cpy.entries);

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      ldgBit: {
        idx: result.valid ? 'verify-lineage-success' : 'verify-lineage-fail',
        val: result.valid ? 1 : 0,
        dat: { verificationResult: result, error: result.failureReason },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};
```

#### 8. `packages/301.spatial/19.ledger.unit/ledger.reduce.ts`

```typescript
import clone from 'clone-deep';
import * as Act from './ledger.action.ts';
import { LedgerModel } from './ledger.model.js';
import * as Buzz from './ledger.buzzer.js';
import type State from '../99.core/state.js';

export function reducer(
  model: LedgerModel = new LedgerModel(),
  act: Act.Actions,
  state?: State
) {
  switch (act.type) {
    case Act.INIT_LEDGER:
      return Buzz.initLedger(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.APPEND_ENTRY:
      return Buzz.appendEntry(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.VERIFY_LINEAGE:
      return Buzz.verifyLineage(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    default:
      return model;
  }
}
```

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { LedgerModel } from '../ledger.model.js';
import {
  canonicalizeJson,
  computeSha256Hex,
  sealLedgerEntry,
  verifyLedgerLineage,
  initLedger,
  appendEntry,
  verifyLineage,
} from '../buz/ledger.buzz.js';
import { GENESIS_HASH_64 } from '../val/ledger.model.js';
import type LedgerBit from '../fce/ledger.bit.js';

describe('19.ledger.unit - Cryptographic Audit Quadruple Ledger', () => {
  let model: LedgerModel;

  beforeEach(() => {
    model = new LedgerModel();
    initLedger(model);
  });

  describe('RFC 8785 JSON Canonicalization Scheme (JCS)', () => {
    it('sorts keys lexicographically by UTF-16 code units', () => {
      const objA = { z: 1, a: 2, m: 3 };
      const objB = { a: 2, m: 3, z: 1 };
      expect(canonicalizeJson(objA)).toBe('{"a":2,"m":3,"z":1}');
      expect(canonicalizeJson(objA)).toBe(canonicalizeJson(objB));
    });

    it('enforces Unicode Normalization Form C (NFC)', () => {
      const decomposed = 'e\u0301'; // e + combining acute
      const precomposed = '\u00e9'; // é
      expect(canonicalizeJson({ text: decomposed })).toBe(canonicalizeJson({ text: precomposed }));
    });

    it('rejects non-finite IEEE-754 numbers with TypeError', () => {
      expect(() => canonicalizeJson({ val: NaN })).toThrow('[CANONICALIZE_ERROR]');
      expect(() => canonicalizeJson({ val: Infinity })).toThrow('[CANONICALIZE_ERROR]');
    });
  });

  describe('Spatial Audit Quadruple Contract & Lineage Sealing', () => {
    const validQuadruple = {
      topologyHash: 'a'.repeat(64),
      overlayHash: 'b'.repeat(64),
      queryHash: 'c'.repeat(64),
      receiptHash: 'd'.repeat(64),
    };

    it('seals Genesis Sequence #1 linked to GENESIS_HASH_64', () => {
      let emittedEntry: any = null;

      const bale: LedgerBit = {
        idx: 'append-1',
        dat: {
          queryType: 'RAYCAST',
          tickNumber: 0,
          streamId: 'stream-alpha',
          auditQuadruple: validQuadruple,
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          emittedEntry = res.ldgBit.dat?.entry;
        },
      };

      appendEntry(model, bale);

      expect(emittedEntry).toBeDefined();
      expect(emittedEntry.sequenceNumber).toBe(1);
      expect(emittedEntry.prevEventHash).toBe(GENESIS_HASH_64);
      expect(emittedEntry.chainedEventHash).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
      expect(model.headSequence).toBe(1);
      expect(model.lastEventHash).toBe(emittedEntry.chainedEventHash);
    });

    it('appends multiple entries in unbroken cryptographic hash chain', () => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const bale: LedgerBit = {
          idx: `append-${i + 1}`,
          dat: {
            queryType: 'BOUNDED_PATH',
            tickNumber: i,
            streamId: 'stream-alpha',
            auditQuadruple: {
              topologyHash: `${i}`.repeat(64),
              overlayHash: 'b'.repeat(64),
              queryHash: 'c'.repeat(64),
              receiptHash: 'd'.repeat(64),
            },
          },
        };
        appendEntry(model, bale);
      }

      expect(model.headSequence).toBe(3);
      expect(model.entries.length).toBe(3);

      const verification = verifyLedgerLineage(model.entries);
      expect(verification.valid).toBe(true);
      expect(verification.totalEntries).toBe(3);
    });

    it('fails closed when an entry payload is tampered', () => {
      // Append two valid entries
      appendEntry(model, {
        idx: 'e1',
        dat: { queryType: 'RAYCAST', tickNumber: 1, streamId: 's', auditQuadruple: validQuadruple },
      });
      appendEntry(model, {
        idx: 'e2',
        dat: { queryType: 'RAYCAST', tickNumber: 2, streamId: 's', auditQuadruple: validQuadruple },
      });

      // Tamper entry 1 tickNumber
      const tamperedEntries = [...model.entries];
      (tamperedEntries[0] as any) = { ...tamperedEntries[0], tickNumber: 999 };

      const result = verifyLedgerLineage(tamperedEntries);
      expect(result.valid).toBe(false);
      expect(result.failureReason).toContain('[CHAIN_HASH_CORRUPTION]');
    });

    it('rejects invalid quadruple hash length or formatting via Zod schema', () => {
      let errorEmitted: any = null;

      const bale: LedgerBit = {
        idx: 'invalid-quad',
        dat: {
          queryType: 'RAYCAST',
          auditQuadruple: {
            topologyHash: 'short-hash',
            overlayHash: 'b'.repeat(64),
            queryHash: 'c'.repeat(64),
            receiptHash: 'd'.repeat(64),
          },
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          errorEmitted = res.ldgBit.dat?.error;
        },
      };

      appendEntry(model, bale);

      expect(errorEmitted).toContain('[QUADRUPLE_SCHEMA_BREACH]');
      expect(model.headSequence).toBe(0);
    });
  });
});
```

```typescript
import type { Model } from '../../99.core/interface/model.interface.js';
import type { PackedAxial, AxialCoord } from '../../38.types.unit/fce/manifest.schema.js';
import type { SpatialAuditQuadruple } from '../../19.ledger.unit/fce/ledger.interface.js';

export type PathFailureClassification = 'UNREACHABLE' | 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED' | 'IMPASSABLE';
export type DisplacementFailureClassification = 'CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED' | 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED' | 'UNREACHABLE';
export type SensoryModality = 'OPTICAL' | 'ACOUSTIC';
export type AxialBearing = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | null;

export interface PathReceiptSuccess {
  readonly ok: true;
  readonly originPacked: PackedAxial;
  readonly destinationPacked: PackedAxial;
  readonly path: readonly PackedAxial[];
  readonly totalCostUnits: number;
  readonly stepsExpanded: number;
  readonly auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadruple;
}

export interface PathReceiptFailure {
  readonly ok: false;
  readonly originPacked: PackedAxial;
  readonly destinationPacked: PackedAxial;
  readonly failureCode: PathFailureClassification;
  readonly stepsExpanded: number;
  readonly auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadruple;
}

export type PathReceipt = PathReceiptSuccess | PathReceiptFailure;

export interface OpticalPerceptEvidence {
  readonly modality: 'OPTICAL';
  readonly transmissionBps: number;
  readonly topologicalDistance: number;
  readonly occludingBarrierCoord: AxialCoord | null;
}

export interface AcousticPerceptEvidence {
  readonly modality: 'ACOUSTIC';
  readonly intensityCdb: number;
  readonly noiseFloorCdb: number;
  readonly snrMarginCdb: number;
  readonly portalHopCount: number;
}

export type PerceptEvidence = OpticalPerceptEvidence | AcousticPerceptEvidence;

export interface PerceptCandidate {
  readonly sourceCoord: AxialCoord;
  readonly observerCoord: AxialCoord;
  readonly qualityScorePerMille: number;
  readonly arrivalBearing: AxialBearing;
  readonly isOccluded: boolean;
  readonly evidence: PerceptEvidence;
  readonly auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadruple;
}

export interface DisplacementReceiptSuccess {
  readonly ok: true;
  readonly targetCellPacked: PackedAxial;
  readonly resolvedCellPacked: PackedAxial;
  readonly relocationCostUnits: number;
  readonly wasEvicted: boolean;
  readonly auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadruple;
}

export interface DisplacementReceiptFailure {
  readonly ok: false;
  readonly targetCellPacked: PackedAxial;
  readonly failureCode: DisplacementFailureClassification;
  readonly auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadruple;
}

export type DisplacementReceipt = DisplacementReceiptSuccess | DisplacementReceiptFailure;

export default interface Receipt extends Model {
  idx: string;
  totalPathReceiptsMinted: number;
  totalPerceptReceiptsMinted: number;
  totalDisplacementReceiptsMinted: number;
  lastMintedTimestamp: number;
}
```

#### 2. `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/val/receipt.model.ts`

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';
import { AxialCoordSchema } from '../../38.types.unit/fce/manifest.schema.js';
import { SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema } from '../../19.ledger.unit/val/ledger.model.js';

export const PackedAxialSchema = z
  .number()
  .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] PackedAxial must be an integer' })
  .min(0, { message: '[OUT_OF_BOUNDS] PackedAxial cannot be negative' })
  .max(0xffffffff, { message: '[OUT_OF_BOUNDS] PackedAxial overflows 32-bit uint' });

export const PathReceiptSuccessSchema = z
  .object({
    ok: z.literal(true),
    originPacked: PackedAxialSchema,
    destinationPacked: PackedAxialSchema,
    path: z.array(PackedAxialSchema),
    totalCostUnits: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] totalCostUnits must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative({ message: '[COST_NEGATIVE] totalCostUnits cannot be negative' }),
    stepsExpanded: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] stepsExpanded must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative(),
    auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema,
  })
  .strict();

export const PathReceiptFailureSchema = z
  .object({
    ok: z.literal(false),
    originPacked: PackedAxialSchema,
    destinationPacked: PackedAxialSchema,
    failureCode: z.enum(['UNREACHABLE', 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 'IMPASSABLE']),
    stepsExpanded: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] stepsExpanded must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative(),
    auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema,
  })
  .strict();

export const PathReceiptSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('ok', [
  PathReceiptSuccessSchema,
  PathReceiptFailureSchema,
]);

export const OpticalPerceptEvidenceSchema = z
  .object({
    modality: z.literal('OPTICAL'),
    transmissionBps: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] transmissionBps must be an integer' })
      .min(0, { message: '[BASIS_POINT_UNDERFLOW] transmissionBps minimum is 0' })
      .max(10000, { message: '[BASIS_POINT_OVERFLOW] transmissionBps maximum is 10000' }),
    topologicalDistance: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] topologicalDistance must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative(),
    occludingBarrierCoord: AxialCoordSchema.nullable(),
  })
  .strict();

export const AcousticPerceptEvidenceSchema = z
  .object({
    modality: z.literal('ACOUSTIC'),
    intensityCdb: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] intensityCdb must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative(),
    noiseFloorCdb: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] noiseFloorCdb must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative(),
    snrMarginCdb: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] snrMarginCdb must be an integer' }),
    portalHopCount: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] portalHopCount must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative(),
  })
  .strict();

export const PerceptEvidenceSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('modality', [
  OpticalPerceptEvidenceSchema,
  AcousticPerceptEvidenceSchema,
]);

export const PerceptCandidateSchema = z
  .object({
    sourceCoord: AxialCoordSchema,
    observerCoord: AxialCoordSchema,
    qualityScorePerMille: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] qualityScorePerMille must be an integer' })
      .min(0, { message: '[PER_MILLE_UNDERFLOW] Minimum quality is 0' })
      .max(1000, { message: '[PER_MILLE_OVERFLOW] Maximum quality is 1000' }),
    arrivalBearing: z
      .union([z.literal(0), z.literal(1), z.literal(2), z.literal(3), z.literal(4), z.literal(5)])
      .nullable(),
    isOccluded: z.boolean(),
    evidence: PerceptEvidenceSchema,
    auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema,
  })
  .strict();

export const DisplacementReceiptSuccessSchema = z
  .object({
    ok: z.literal(true),
    targetCellPacked: PackedAxialSchema,
    resolvedCellPacked: PackedAxialSchema,
    relocationCostUnits: z
      .number()
      .int({ message: '[FLOAT_PROHIBITED] relocationCostUnits must be an integer' })
      .nonnegative(),
    wasEvicted: z.boolean(),
    auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema,
  })
  .strict();

export const DisplacementReceiptFailureSchema = z
  .object({
    ok: z.literal(false),
    targetCellPacked: PackedAxialSchema,
    failureCode: z.enum(['CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED', 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 'UNREACHABLE']),
    auditQuadruple: SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema,
  })
  .strict();

export const DisplacementReceiptSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('ok', [
  DisplacementReceiptSuccessSchema,
  DisplacementReceiptFailureSchema,
]);
```

#### 6. `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/buz/receipt.buzz.ts`

```typescript
import { ReceiptModel } from '../receipt.model.js';
import type ReceiptBit from '../fce/receipt.bit.js';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';
import type {
  PathReceipt,
  PerceptCandidate,
  DisplacementReceipt,
} from '../fce/receipt.interface.js';
import {
  PathReceiptSchema,
  PerceptCandidateSchema,
  DisplacementReceiptSchema,
} from '../val/receipt.model.js';

/**
 * Deep freezes an object recursively to guarantee plain data immutability.
 */
export function deepFreezeReceipt<T>(obj: T): T {
  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') return obj;
  Object.freeze(obj);
  for (const key of Object.getOwnPropertyNames(obj)) {
    const val = (obj as any)[key];
    if (val !== null && typeof val === 'object' && !Object.isFrozen(val)) {
      deepFreezeReceipt(val);
    }
  }
  return obj;
}

export const initReceipt = (
  cpy: ReceiptModel,
  bal?: ReceiptBit,
  ste?: State
): ReceiptModel => {
  cpy.totalPathReceiptsMinted = 0;
  cpy.totalPerceptReceiptsMinted = 0;
  cpy.totalDisplacementReceiptsMinted = 0;
  cpy.lastMintedTimestamp = 0;

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ rcpBit: { idx: 'init-receipt-success', val: 1 } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const mintPathReceipt = (
  cpy: ReceiptModel,
  bal?: ReceiptBit,
  ste?: State
): ReceiptModel => {
  const candidate = bal?.dat?.pathReceipt;
  const parsed = PathReceiptSchema.safeParse(candidate);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        rcpBit: {
          idx: 'mint-path-receipt-fail',
          val: 0,
          dat: { error: `[PATH_RECEIPT_SCHEMA_BREACH] ${parsed.error.errors.map((e) => e.message).join('; ')}` },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const sealedReceipt = deepFreezeReceipt(parsed.data as PathReceipt);
  cpy.totalPathReceiptsMinted += 1;
  cpy.lastMintedTimestamp = Date.now();

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      rcpBit: {
        idx: 'mint-path-receipt-success',
        val: 1,
        dat: { pathReceipt: sealedReceipt },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const mintPerceptReceipt = (
  cpy: ReceiptModel,
  bal?: ReceiptBit,
  ste?: State
): ReceiptModel => {
  const candidate = bal?.dat?.perceptCandidate;
  const parsed = PerceptCandidateSchema.safeParse(candidate);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        rcpBit: {
          idx: 'mint-percept-receipt-fail',
          val: 0,
          dat: { error: `[PERCEPT_RECEIPT_SCHEMA_BREACH] ${parsed.error.errors.map((e) => e.message).join('; ')}` },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const sealedCandidate = deepFreezeReceipt(parsed.data as PerceptCandidate);
  cpy.totalPerceptReceiptsMinted += 1;
  cpy.lastMintedTimestamp = Date.now();

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      rcpBit: {
        idx: 'mint-percept-receipt-success',
        val: 1,
        dat: { perceptCandidate: sealedCandidate },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const mintDisplacementReceipt = (
  cpy: ReceiptModel,
  bal?: ReceiptBit,
  ste?: State
): ReceiptModel => {
  const candidate = bal?.dat?.displacementReceipt;
  const parsed = DisplacementReceiptSchema.safeParse(candidate);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        rcpBit: {
          idx: 'mint-displacement-receipt-fail',
          val: 0,
          dat: { error: `[DISPLACEMENT_RECEIPT_SCHEMA_BREACH] ${parsed.error.errors.map((e) => e.message).join('; ')}` },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const sealedDisplacement = deepFreezeReceipt(parsed.data as DisplacementReceipt);
  cpy.totalDisplacementReceiptsMinted += 1;
  cpy.lastMintedTimestamp = Date.now();

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      rcpBit: {
        idx: 'mint-displacement-receipt-success',
        val: 1,
        dat: { displacementReceipt: sealedDisplacement },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};
```

```typescript
export {
  deepFreezeReceipt,
  initReceipt,
  mintPathReceipt,
  mintPerceptReceipt,
  mintDisplacementReceipt,
} from './buz/receipt.buzz.js';
```

#### 8. `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/receipt.reduce.ts`

```typescript
import clone from 'clone-deep';
import * as Act from './receipt.action.js';
import { ReceiptModel } from './receipt.model.js';
import * as Buzz from './receipt.buzzer.js';
import type State from '../99.core/state.js';

export function reducer(
  model: ReceiptModel = new ReceiptModel(),
  act: Act.Actions,
  state?: State
) {
  switch (act.type) {
    case Act.INIT_RECEIPT:
      return Buzz.initReceipt(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.MINT_PATH_RECEIPT:
      return Buzz.mintPathReceipt(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.MINT_PERCEPT_RECEIPT:
      return Buzz.mintPerceptReceipt(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.MINT_DISPLACEMENT_RECEIPT:
      return Buzz.mintDisplacementReceipt(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    default:
      return model;
  }
}
```

#### 9. `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/receipt.unit.ts`

```typescript
import type { Unit } from '../99.core/interface/unit.interface.js';
import { ReceiptModel } from './receipt.model.js';
import { reducer } from './receipt.reduce.js';

export default class ReceiptUnit implements Unit<ReceiptModel> {
  idx: string = '42.receipt';
  model: ReceiptModel = new ReceiptModel();
  reducer = reducer;
}
```

#### 10. `packages/301.spatial/42.receipt.unit/test/receipt.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { ReceiptModel } from '../receipt.model.js';
import {
  initReceipt,
  mintPathReceipt,
  mintPerceptReceipt,
  mintDisplacementReceipt,
} from '../buz/receipt.buzz.js';
import type ReceiptBit from '../fce/receipt.bit.js';

describe('42.receipt.unit - Sealed Plain Data Receipts', () => {
  let model: ReceiptModel;

  const validQuadruple = {
    topologyHash: 'a'.repeat(64),
    overlayHash: 'b'.repeat(64),
    queryHash: 'c'.repeat(64),
    receiptHash: 'd'.repeat(64),
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    model = new ReceiptModel();
    initReceipt(model);
  });

  describe('Path Receipt Construction & Invariants', () => {
    it('mints a valid success path receipt with frozen immutability', () => {
      let emittedReceipt: any = null;

      const bale: ReceiptBit = {
        idx: 'path-success',
        dat: {
          pathReceipt: {
            ok: true,
            originPacked: 2147516416,
            destinationPacked: 2147581952,
            path: [2147516416, 2147581952],
            totalCostUnits: 250,
            stepsExpanded: 12,
            auditQuadruple: validQuadruple,
          },
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          emittedReceipt = res.rcpBit.dat?.pathReceipt;
        },
      };

      mintPathReceipt(model, bale);

      expect(emittedReceipt).toBeDefined();
      expect(emittedReceipt.ok).toBe(true);
      expect(emittedReceipt.totalCostUnits).toBe(250);
      expect(Object.isFrozen(emittedReceipt)).toBe(true);
      expect(Object.isFrozen(emittedReceipt.path)).toBe(true);
      expect(model.totalPathReceiptsMinted).toBe(1);
    });

    it('rejects floating-point costs with FLOAT_PROHIBITED', () => {
      let errorEmitted: any = null;

      const bale: ReceiptBit = {
        idx: 'path-float-fail',
        dat: {
          pathReceipt: {
            ok: true,
            originPacked: 0,
            destinationPacked: 1,
            path: [0, 1],
            totalCostUnits: 12.5,
            stepsExpanded: 2,
            auditQuadruple: validQuadruple,
          } as any,
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          errorEmitted = res.rcpBit.dat?.error;
        },
      };

      mintPathReceipt(model, bale);

      expect(errorEmitted).toContain('[FLOAT_PROHIBITED]');
      expect(model.totalPathReceiptsMinted).toBe(0);
    });
  });

  describe('Sensory Evidence Percept Receipt Specification', () => {
    it('mints optical percept evidence within strict basis-point bounds', () => {
      let emittedCandidate: any = null;

      const bale: ReceiptBit = {
        idx: 'percept-optical',
        dat: {
          perceptCandidate: {
            sourceCoord: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
            observerCoord: { q: 2, r: -2, s: 0 },
            qualityScorePerMille: 850,
            arrivalBearing: 2,
            isOccluded: false,
            evidence: {
              modality: 'OPTICAL',
              transmissionBps: 9200,
              topologicalDistance: 2,
              occludingBarrierCoord: null,
            },
            auditQuadruple: validQuadruple,
          },
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          emittedCandidate = res.rcpBit.dat?.perceptCandidate;
        },
      };

      mintPerceptReceipt(model, bale);

      expect(emittedCandidate).toBeDefined();
      expect(emittedCandidate.evidence.modality).toBe('OPTICAL');
      expect(emittedCandidate.evidence.transmissionBps).toBe(9200);
      expect(Object.isFrozen(emittedCandidate)).toBe(true);
      expect(model.totalPerceptReceiptsMinted).toBe(1);
    });

    it('rejects optical evidence exceeding 10000 bps', () => {
      let errorEmitted: any = null;

      const bale: ReceiptBit = {
        idx: 'percept-overflow',
        dat: {
          perceptCandidate: {
            sourceCoord: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
            observerCoord: { q: 1, r: -1, s: 0 },
            qualityScorePerMille: 500,
            arrivalBearing: 1,
            isOccluded: false,
            evidence: {
              modality: 'OPTICAL',
              transmissionBps: 10001,
              topologicalDistance: 1,
              occludingBarrierCoord: null,
            },
            auditQuadruple: validQuadruple,
          } as any,
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          errorEmitted = res.rcpBit.dat?.error;
        },
      };

      mintPerceptReceipt(model, bale);

      expect(errorEmitted).toContain('[BASIS_POINT_OVERFLOW]');
      expect(model.totalPerceptReceiptsMinted).toBe(0);
    });

    it('mints acoustic percept evidence with SNR margin in cdB', () => {
      let emittedCandidate: any = null;

      const bale: ReceiptBit = {
        idx: 'percept-acoustic',
        dat: {
          perceptCandidate: {
            sourceCoord: { q: 1, r: 1, s: -2 },
            observerCoord: { q: 0, r: 0, s: 0 },
            qualityScorePerMille: 600,
            arrivalBearing: 4,
            isOccluded: true,
            evidence: {
              modality: 'ACOUSTIC',
              intensityCdb: 6500,
              noiseFloorCdb: 4200,
              snrMarginCdb: 2300,
              portalHopCount: 1,
            },
            auditQuadruple: validQuadruple,
          },
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          emittedCandidate = res.rcpBit.dat?.perceptCandidate;
        },
      };

      mintPerceptReceipt(model, bale);

      expect(emittedCandidate).toBeDefined();
      expect(emittedCandidate.evidence.modality).toBe('ACOUSTIC');
      expect(emittedCandidate.evidence.snrMarginCdb).toBe(2300);
      expect(Object.isFrozen(emittedCandidate)).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe('Displacement Result Formulation', () => {
    it('mints displacement success with legal cell relocation', () => {
      let emittedDisplacement: any = null;

      const bale: ReceiptBit = {
        idx: 'displacement-ok',
        dat: {
          displacementReceipt: {
            ok: true,
            targetCellPacked: 2147516416,
            resolvedCellPacked: 2147516417,
            relocationCostUnits: 100,
            wasEvicted: true,
            auditQuadruple: validQuadruple,
          },
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          emittedDisplacement = res.rcpBit.dat?.displacementReceipt;
        },
      };

      mintDisplacementReceipt(model, bale);

      expect(emittedDisplacement).toBeDefined();
      expect(emittedDisplacement.ok).toBe(true);
      expect(emittedDisplacement.wasEvicted).toBe(true);
      expect(Object.isFrozen(emittedDisplacement)).toBe(true);
      expect(model.totalDisplacementReceiptsMinted).toBe(1);
    });

    it('rejects displacement receipt with un-schemaed narrative properties', () => {
      let errorEmitted: any = null;

      const bale: ReceiptBit = {
        idx: 'displacement-dirty',
        dat: {
          displacementReceipt: {
            ok: false,
            targetCellPacked: 2147516416,
            failureCode: 'CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED',
            auditQuadruple: validQuadruple,
            actorId: 'char_bog',
          } as any,
        },
        slv: (res) => {
          errorEmitted = res.rcpBit.dat?.error;
        },
      };

      mintDisplacementReceipt(model, bale);

      expect(errorEmitted).toContain('[DISPLACEMENT_RECEIPT_SCHEMA_BREACH]');
      expect(model.totalDisplacementReceiptsMinted).toBe(0);
    });
  });
});
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### User Story

In the wiregrass scrubbing room of the lower basin works, an opposed confrontation had escalated between Bog and the reclamation foreman over a misplaced heavy wrench. As the simulation engine ticked toward mechanical resolution, the physics oracle completed its A* traversal pass and sensory line-of-sight sweep. In earlier prototypes, the spatial subsystem returned mutable class instances with attached helper methods, allowing downstream adapters to modify traversal costs mid-flight or inject character emotional lore directly into the spatial coordinates. The resulting timeline suffered subtle, non-deterministic state drift: one downstream resolver saw a path cost of 250 centiseconds, while another mutated the path object in memory to 180 centiseconds, breaking the Merkle hash chain and causing the simulation engine to crash during state settlement.

With **Phase 4 Sealed Plain Data Receipts In 42.receipt.unit** active, that failure mode is permanently eradicated. The moment the spatial kernel finishes evaluating a bounded path, an optical line-of-sight raycast, an acoustic wavefront hop, or an overcrowding eviction, the raw mathematical result is handed to `42.receipt.unit`. Here, the payload is parsed against strict, closed Zod contracts, bound inextricably to its 64-character SHA-256 cryptographic audit quadruple ($H_{\text{topology}}, H_{\text{overlay}}, H_{\text{query}}, H_{\text{receipt}}$), and subjected to recursive deep freezing. When the simulation engine receives the `PathReceipt` or `PerceptCandidate`, it receives an unalterable, frozen crystal of pure physical truth. The receipt contains no character names, no narrative bias, and zero mutable memory references: it is an immutable receipt of spatial fact that can be audited, replayed, and verified across decades of canon history without a single bit of drift.

#### Strategic Goal

Supports the foundational system mandate of **Deterministic Spatial Proof Integrity**: producing sealed, immutable plain-data receipts that bind mathematical outcomes directly to cryptographic state hashes at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$, guaranteeing zero in-memory state mutation, zero narrative contamination, and 100% bit-exact replayability across all client and worker runtimes.

#### Context & Value

- **The Strategic Value:** Acts as the clean separation layer between the stateless spatial oracle and the stateful simulation ledger. By packaging all geometric and sensory calculations into sealed, deep-frozen data contracts, the architecture guarantees that physical evaluations cannot be tampered with by downstream LLM authoring tools, dramatic staging accumulators, or multi-threaded worker isolates.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Constructing discriminated union schemas that model both positive calculation proofs and explicit failure classifications (`UNREACHABLE`, `BUDGET_EXCEEDED`, `IMPASSABLE`, `CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED`) with strict `.strict()` closure, enforcing integer basis-point ($0\text{--}10{,}000\text{ bps}$) and centi-decibel ($\text{cdB}$) bounds without allowing JavaScript floating-point conversions, and recursively freezing deeply nested arrays and coordinate records with zero memory leak overhead.

#### Interface

- **`000.series-sower-engine` (Simulation Runtime Host):** Ingests sealed `PathReceipt`, `PerceptCandidate`, and `DisplacementReceipt` payloads across the reactive event bus, translating physical step counts and acoustic SNR margins into narrative belief records, somatic stamina deductions, and scene staging constraints.
- **`packages/301.spatial/19.ledger.unit` (Cryptographic Lineage Ledger):** Supplies the `SpatialAuditQuadrupleSchema` that binds every minted receipt to its immutable hash quadruple ($H_{\text{topology}}, H_{\text{overlay}}, H_{\text{query}}, H_{\text{receipt}}$), anchoring the receipt into the append-only event stream.
- **`packages/301.spatial/38.types.unit` (Geometric Primitive Encoders):** Supplies `AxialCoordSchema` and `PackedAxial` unsigned 32-bit integer bitmasks to define all coordinate endpoints and path sequences within receipts.
- **`packages/301.spatial/00.spatial.unit` (Core Geometric Kernel):** Dispatches raw geometric search outputs into `42.receipt.unit` buzzers to transform unvalidated execution data into certified, frozen receipts.

#### Interaction

- **Blessed Terminal Interface (TUI Flight Deck):**
  - In the curses cockpit (`apps/995.library` running `packages/301.spatial/98.menu.unit`), selecting `[04] MINT SEALED SPATIAL RECEIPTS` or `[RECEIPT MENU] -> AUDIT PLAIN DATA CONTRACTS` triggers the execution buzzer in `42.receipt.unit`.
  - The buzzer exercises receipt generation across synthetic test vectors: minting a valid multi-node path receipt, minting optical percept evidence with transmission basis points, minting acoustic evidence with noise floor margins, and injecting synthetic float and narrative contaminations to assert fail-closed rejection.
  - Telemetry receipts stream in real time into console pane `cns00` in pure 7-bit ASCII:
    ```plaintext
    >> [SPATIAL::RECEIPT] Initializing 42.receipt.unit contract audit...
    >> [RECEIPT::PATH] Minting 12-step path receipt (totalCost: 250 units)... [OK]
    >> [RECEIPT::FREEZE] Asserting recursive deep-freeze immutability... [OK]
    >> [RECEIPT::PERCEPT] Minting optical evidence (9200 bps transmission)... [OK]
    >> [RECEIPT::ACOUSTIC] Minting acoustic evidence (+2300 cdB SNR margin)... [OK]
    >> [RECEIPT::DISPLACE] Minting eviction result (target -> legal cell)... [OK]
    >> [RECEIPT::TRAP] Negative float trap (12.5 cost units) -> FLOAT_PROHIBITED [TRAPPED]
    >> [RECEIPT::STATUS] All 42.receipt.unit plain-data schemas certified green at T_0 = $0.00.
    ```
  - Any schema violation or un-frozen return triggers an immediate red error banner (`:: [FAIL] [RECEIPT_SCHEMA_BREACH]`) in `cns00`, halting test gauntlets before invalid receipts can be committed to downstream ledgers.

```typescript
import type { Model } from '../../99.core/interface/model.interface.js';
import type {
  SpatialPort,
  SpatialExecutionContext,
  SynchronousExecutionReceipt,
  ScrubbedRaycastQuery,
  ScrubbedRoutingQuery,
  ScrubbedReachabilityQuery,
  ScrubbedDisplacementQuery,
  ScrubbedSpatialQuery,
} from '../../14.port.unit/fce/port.interface.js';
import type { MergedTopologySnapshot } from '../../38.types.unit/fce/overlay.schema.js';
import type { PathReceipt, PerceptCandidate, DisplacementReceipt } from '../../42.receipt.unit/fce/receipt.interface.js';

export interface KernelPathResult {
  readonly ok: boolean;
  readonly path: readonly number[];
  readonly totalCostUnits: number;
  readonly cellsExpanded: number;
  readonly failureCode?: 'UNREACHABLE' | 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED' | 'IMPASSABLE';
}

export interface KernelRaycastResult {
  readonly ok: boolean;
  readonly transmissionBps: number;
  readonly topologicalDistance: number;
  readonly isOccluded: boolean;
  readonly occludingCoord: { readonly q: number; readonly r: number; readonly s: number } | null;
}

export interface KernelDisplacementResult {
  readonly ok: boolean;
  readonly targetCellPacked: number;
  readonly resolvedCellPacked: number;
  readonly relocationCostUnits: number;
  readonly wasEvicted: boolean;
  readonly failureCode?: 'CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED' | 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED' | 'UNREACHABLE';
}

export interface PureSpatialKernel {
  readonly kernelVersion: string;
  executeRaycast(query: ScrubbedRaycastQuery, topology?: MergedTopologySnapshot): KernelRaycastResult;
  executeBoundedPath(query: ScrubbedRoutingQuery, topology?: MergedTopologySnapshot): KernelPathResult;
  executeReachability(query: ScrubbedReachabilityQuery, topology?: MergedTopologySnapshot): readonly number[];
  executeDisplacement(query: ScrubbedDisplacementQuery, topology?: MergedTopologySnapshot): KernelDisplacementResult;
}

export default interface Spatial extends Model {
  idx: string;
  kernelVersion: string;
  totalSynchronousInvocations: number;
  totalBudgetsTripped: number;
  lastExecutionMicros: number;
}
```
